=== EARTH ROTATION LOG T15 (CW+CCW v jednom behu, bez re-homingu) ===
=================================================================
EARTH ROTATION MEASUREMENT T15 - CW + CCW BLOK V JEDNOM BEHU
NEMA17 + A4988 + ADXRS290 + MPU6050 (kontrola naklonu)
=================================================================

POZOR: pozice A (azimut 0 deg) musi byt pred spustenim nastavena
RUCNE (osa X miri na sever), sestava vyrovnana a od ted uz s ni
NEHYBAT. Vsechny dalsi presuny dela vyhradne motor. Homing se
mezi CW a CCW blokem NEOPAKUJE - motor zustava zapnuty po celou
dobu obou bloku, aby se do srovnani CW/CCW nedostala nejistota
z ruceniho mireni.

Initializing SPI1...
  ADXRS290: ADI=0xad MEMS=0x1d DEV=0x92
  ADXRS290 measurement mode ON, FILTER=0x00 (raw, bez filtru)

Initializing I2C0 (MPU6050)...
  MPU6050: WHO_AM_I=0x70

=================================================================
BLOK: smer=CW  (CW_SIGN=+1)
AZIMUTH_STEP_DEG = 22.5  (16 azimutu)
NUM_REPEATS_PER_DIR = 15
APPROACH_OVERSHOOT_DEG = 15.0, APPROACH_SIGN = +1
FIXNI settle = 4.0s, FIXNI trim = 50
=================================================================

Celkem mereni v tomto bloku: 240
